def delete_all_sources():
    """
    Delete all configured sources.

    With no sources remaining, every collected
    config becomes orphaned and is removed.
    """

    data = _load()

    deleted = len(
        data["sources"]
    )

    data["sources"] = []

    _save(
        data
    )

    delete_all_configs()

    return deleted

def recover_expired() -> int:

    dirs()
    n=0
    t=now()

    with Lock():

        for p in list(
            LEASED.glob("*.json")
        ):
            o=read(p)

            if not o:
                continue

            if int(
                o.get(
                    "lease_until_epoch",
                    0,
                )
            ) > t:
                continue

def without_keys(
    value: dict[str, Any],
    ignored_keys: Iterable[str],
) -> dict[str, Any]:

    ignored = set(
        ignored_keys
    )

    return {
        key: deepcopy(item)
        for key, item
        in value.items()
        if key not in ignored
    }

--- publish/filter.py ---
     1	from __future__ import annotations
     2	
     3	import json
     4	
     5	from dataclasses import dataclass
     6	from pathlib import Path
     7	from typing import Any
     8	
     9	
    10	CONFIG_DIR = Path(
    11	    "/var/lib/config-location/configs"
    12	)
    13	
    14	POLICY_PATH = Path(
    15	    "/var/lib/config-location/"
    16	    "health-lifecycle/"
    17	    "policy-latest.json"
    18	)
    19	
    20	
    21	ALLOWED_STATES = {
    22	    "healthy",
    23	    "recovered",
    24	}
    25	
    26	
    27	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28	class PublishSnapshot:
    29	
    30	    policy_available: bool
    31	
    32	    total_configs: int
    33	    policy_tracked: int
    34	
    35	    publishable: int
    36	    suppressed: int
    37	
    38	    missing_policy_record: int
    39	
    40	    configs: tuple[
    41	        dict[str, Any],
    42	        ...
    43	    ]
    44	
    45	
    46	def _read_json(
    47	    path: Path,
    48	) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    49	
    50	    try:
    51	
    52	        obj = json.loads(
    53	            path.read_text(
    54	                encoding="utf-8"
    55	            )
    56	        )
    57	
    58	        if isinstance(
    59	            obj,
    60	            dict,
    61	        ):
    62	            return obj
    63	
    64	    except Exception:
    65	        pass
    66	
    67	    return None
    68	
    69	
    70	def _load_configs() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    71	
    72	    configs = {}
    73	
    74	    for path in CONFIG_DIR.glob(
    75	        "*.json"
    76	    ):
    77	
    78	        obj = _read_json(
    79	            path
    80	        )
    81	
    82	        if not obj:
    83	            continue
    84	
    85	        config_id = str(
    86	            obj.get(
    87	                "id"
    88	            )
    89	            or path.stem
    90	        )
    91	
    92	        raw = obj.get(
    93	            "raw"
    94	        )
    95	
    96	        if not isinstance(
    97	            raw,
    98	            str,
    99	        ):
   100	            continue
   101	
   102	        configs[
   103	            config_id
   104	        ] = obj
   105	
   106	    return configs
   107	
   108	
   109	def _policy_index(
   110	    value: dict[str, Any],
   111	) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   112	
   113	    result = {}
   114	
   115	    decisions = value.get(
   116	        "decisions",
   117	        [],
   118	    )
   119	
   120	    if not isinstance(
   121	        decisions,
   122	        list,
   123	    ):
   124	        return result
   125	
   126	
   127	    for item in decisions:
   128	
   129	        if not isinstance(
   130	            item,
   131	            dict,
   132	        ):
   133	            continue
   134	
   135	        config_id = str(
   136	            item.get(
   137	                "config_id",
   138	                "",
   139	            )
   140	        )
   141	
   142	        if not config_id:
   143	            continue
   144	
   145	        result[
   146	            config_id
   147	        ] = item
   148	
   149	
   150	    return result
   151	
   152	
   153	def publishable_config_ids() -> set[str]:
   154	
   155	    policy = _read_json(
   156	        POLICY_PATH
   157	    )
   158	
   159	    if not policy:
   160	        # Fail closed.
   161	        return set()
   162	
   163	
   164	    index = _policy_index(
   165	        policy
   166	    )
   167	
   168	
   169	    allowed = set()
   170	
   171	    for config_id, item in (
   172	        index.items()
   173	    ):
   174	
   175	        state = str(
   176	            item.get(
   177	                "policy_state",
   178	                "",
   179	            )
   180	        ).strip().lower()
   181	
   182	        publish_flag = bool(
   183	            item.get(
   184	                "publish_eligible",
   185	                False,
   186	            )
   187	        )
   188	
   189	
   190	        if (
   191	            state in ALLOWED_STATES
   192	            and publish_flag
   193	        ):
   194	
   195	            allowed.add(
   196	                config_id
   197	            )
   198	
   199	
   200	    return allowed
   201	
   202	
   203	def build_publish_snapshot(
   204	    *,
   205	    config_type: str | None = None,
   206	) -> PublishSnapshot:
   207	
   208	    configs = _load_configs()
   209	
   210	    policy = _read_json(
   211	        POLICY_PATH
   212	    )
   213	
   214	
   215	    if not policy:
   216	
   217	        return PublishSnapshot(
   218	            policy_available=False,
   219	
   220	            total_configs=len(
   221	                configs
   222	            ),
   223	
   224	            policy_tracked=0,
   225	
   226	            publishable=0,
   227	
   228	            suppressed=len(
   229	                configs
   230	            ),
   231	
   232	            missing_policy_record=len(
   233	                configs
   234	            ),
   235	
   236	            configs=(),
   237	        )
   238	
   239	
   240	    policy_index = _policy_index(
   241	        policy
   242	    )
   243	
   244	
   245	    output = []
   246	
   247	    missing_policy_record = 0
   248	
   249	
   250	    kind_filter = (
   251	        str(
   252	            config_type
   253	        )
   254	        .strip()
   255	        .lower()
   256	        if config_type
   257	        else None
   258	    )
   259	
   260	
   261	    for config_id, record in (
   262	        configs.items()
   263	    ):
   264	
   265	        kind = str(
   266	            record.get(
   267	                "type",
   268	                "",
   269	            )
   270	        ).strip().lower()
   271	
   272	
   273	        if (
   274	            kind_filter
   275	            and kind != kind_filter
   276	        ):
   277	            continue
   278	
   279	
   280	        decision = (
   281	            policy_index.get(
   282	                config_id
   283	            )
   284	        )
   285	
   286	
   287	        if decision is None:
   288	
   289	            missing_policy_record += 1
   290	
   291	            # Fail closed:
   292	            # never expose an untested/unclassified
   293	            # config.
   294	            continue
   295	
   296	
   297	        state = str(
   298	            decision.get(
   299	                "policy_state",
   300	                "",
   301	            )
   302	        ).strip().lower()
   303	
   304	
   305	        eligible = bool(
   306	            decision.get(
   307	                "publish_eligible",
   308	                False,
   309	            )
   310	        )
   311	
   312	
   313	        if (
   314	            state not in ALLOWED_STATES
   315	            or not eligible
   316	        ):
   317	
   318	            continue
   319	
   320	
   321	        output.append(
   322	            record
   323	        )
   324	
   325	
   326	    # Deterministic order prevents random output
   327	    # churn between identical requests.
   328	    output.sort(
   329	        key=lambda x: (
   330	            str(
   331	                x.get(
   332	                    "type",
   333	                    "",
   334	                )
   335	            ),
   336	            str(
   337	                x.get(
   338	                    "id",
   339	                    "",
   340	                )
   341	            ),
   342	        )
   343	    )
   344	
   345	
   346	    relevant_total = sum(
   347	        1
   348	        for record in configs.values()
   349	        if (
   350	            not kind_filter
   351	            or str(
   352	                record.get(
   353	                    "type",
   354	                    "",
   355	                )
   356	            ).strip().lower()
   357	            == kind_filter
   358	        )
   359	    )
   360	
   361	
   362	    return PublishSnapshot(
   363	        policy_available=True,
   364	
   365	        total_configs=relevant_total,
   366	
   367	        policy_tracked=len(
   368	            policy_index
   369	        ),
   370	
   371	        publishable=len(
   372	            output
   373	        ),
   374	
   375	        suppressed=max(

Q1. What is the canonical lifecycle state object?
Q2. Where is lifecycle state persisted?
Q3. What event/function moves a config into quarantine?
Q4. What event/function permanently removes a config?
Q5. Is removal based on age, health failures, or both?
Q6. What exact timestamp is used for config lifetime?
Q7. Is lifetime measured from first_seen, fetched_at, created_at, or another field?
Q8. Is config_lifetime already wired to Central Settings?
Q9. What consecutive-failure rule exists before quarantine/removal?
Q10. Does Publish immediately exclude quarantined/expired/removed configs?
Q11. Does Country clean state when a config is removed?
Q12. What cleanup jobs already remove orphan state/history?
Q13. What systemd service owns lifecycle/removal today?
Q14. Is there a lock/cursor preventing duplicate removals?
Q15. What is the safest insertion point for a real lifecycle engine?
